数据层</a><h1>数据架构：结构、传递与生命周期</h1><p>这里集中回答：数据是什么、谁生产、谁消费、如何传递、何时有效、何时失效、由谁保存和释放。</p><nav><a href="#lifecycles">参数/图像/结果深追</a><a href="#structure">数据结构</a><a href="#flows">传递总图</a><a href="#model">方案、流程、节点与资源</a><a href="#binding">端口、类型、订阅与索引</a><a href="#runtime">单轮输入、变量与执行结果</a><a href="#image">图像、ROI、几何与显示载荷</a><a href="#publication">回调、缓存、线程与界面发布</a><a href="#history">运行历史、动作历史与快照</a><a href="#persistence">文件格式、资源路径与保存事务</a><a href="#boundary">跨层消息、ABI与返回数据</a></nav><p class="notice">专题已建立完整分析维度；原版恢复仍不完整。橙色为原版源码确认，蓝色为当前独立实现，未知单列。两者不是一一对应类图，图像指针、ABI布局和.sol格式不以当前实现替代。</p><div class="dimensions"><span>身份与层级</span><span>字段/类型/基数</span><span>数据来源与消费者</span><span>引用/复制/序列化</span><span>索引与类型转换</span><span>更新/覆盖/失效</span><span>缓存与容量</span><span>线程与发布顺序</span><span>所有权/释放/生命周期</span><span>错误/取消/部分提交</span><span>持久化/版本/资源路径</span><span>原版与当前差异</span></div><section id="lifecycles"><h2>参数、图像、模块结果：逐层传递、缓存与释放</h2><p class="notice">以下是新增的实质取证：50个原版方法体、19个原/当前数据生命周期切片。清字典、解除固定、关闭映射、释放结果容器和移除显示引用分别记录。未知分支尚未恢复，不能据此宣称全部释放安全。</p><article id="lifecycle-parameters"><h3>参数：编辑、订阅、执行参数缓存和清理</h3><details class="fact" id="trace-DP01"><summary>DP01 · ModuleParam[string key] → ModuleManager.SetParamValue / SetDynamicRunParamValue</summary><p class="source-confirmed">source-confirmed</p><table><tbody><tr><th>数据表示 / 复制或借用</th><td>key/value字符串；模块ID；Group的%键可拆索引</td></tr><tr><th>缓存与失效</th><td>getter即时读SDK；setter并非仅改UI缓存</td></tr><tr><th>释放者与时机</th><td>非全局先记录历史，再SDK写，成功后通知；没有这里的统一回滚</td></tr></tbody></table><p>Parent.IsOnDispose时不读写；全局模块setter直接SDK写后return。普通模块先RecordParamUnRedo再SetValueBySDK，最后OnModuleParamChanged。Group且key以%开头走动态API；其余走普通参数API并要求bThrowException:true。SDK写失败时通知尚未发生，但历史捕获可能已发生。</p><details><summary>源码证据 	{
		get
		{
			string strValue = &quot;&quot;;
			if (!Parent.IsOnDispose)
			{
				if (Parent is VmModuleSet &amp;&amp; key.StartsWith(&quot;%&quot;))
				{
					string[] array = key.Split(&#39;,&#39;);
					ServerSDKManager.serverSDKManager.mModuleManager.GetDynamicRunParamValue(Parent.ID, array[0], ref strValue, bThrowException: true, (array.Count() &gt; 1) ? array[1] : &quot;&quot;);
				}
				else
				{
					ServerSDKManager.serverSDKManager.mModuleManager.GetParamValue(Parent.ID, key, ref strValue, bThrowException: true);
				}
			}
			return strValue;
		}
		set
		{
			if (!Parent.IsOnDispose)
			{
				if (Parent.IsGlobalModu)
				{
					SetValueBySDK(key, value);
					return;
				}
				RecordParamUnRedo(key);
				SetValueBySDK(key, value);
				Parent.OnModuleParamChanged(key);
			}
		}
	}

{
    private readonly byte[] pixels;
    public int Width { get; }
    public int Height { get; }
    public int Stride=&gt;Width;
    public ImagePixelFormat Format=&gt;ImagePixelFormat.Mono8;
    public ReadOnlySpan&lt;byte&gt; Pixels =&gt; pixels;
    public byte this[int x, int y] =&gt; pixels[Offset(x, y)];
    private int Offset(int x, int y)
    {
        if ((uint)x &gt;= (uint)Width || (uint)y &gt;= (uint)Height) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
        return y * Width + x;
    }
    public GrayImage(int width, int height, ReadOnlySpan&lt;byte&gt; data)
    {
        if (width &lt;= 0 || height &lt;= 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(width));
        if (data.Length != checked(width * height)) throw new ArgumentException(&quot;Pixel count must equal width * height.&quot;, nameof(data));
        Width = width; Height = height; pixels = data.ToArray();
    }
}

	{
		if (!IsSuitable(target.TypeName, source.TypeName))
		{
			return;
		}
		if (target.IsLeafNode &amp;&amp; source.IsLeafNode)
		{
			ImvsSdkDefine.IMVS_SUBSCRIBE_BASIC_INFO subInfo = GetSubInfo(source);
			ImvsSdkDefine.IMVS_PUBLISH_BASIC_INFO pubInfo = GetPubInfo(target, source.RelateIndex);
			ServerSDKManager.serverSDKManager.mModuleManager.SubscribeResult(subInfo, pubInfo);
			return;
		}
		if (source.Childs.Count == target.Childs.Count)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i &lt; target.Childs.Count; i++)
			{
				source.Childs[i].RelateIO = target.Childs[i];
			}
			return;
		}
		string lang = AppLocalizationService.GetLang(&quot;SubscriptionsNotAligned&quot;);
		throw new Exception(lang);
	}</pre><p>analysis/decompiled/VM.Core/VM.Core/VmModule.cs · 1853–1868</p><pre>	internal virtual void UnSubscribe(VmIO source, VmIO target)

        async Task&lt;ExecutionSessionResult&gt; Execute(string id,IReadOnlyDictionary&lt;string,PortValue&gt;? arguments,CancellationToken token,int depth,int? groupLoopIndex=null){
            if(depth&gt;32)throw new InvalidDataException(&quot;Independent subprogram depth limit is 32.&quot;);
            var procedure=document.Procedures.SingleOrDefault(p=&gt;p.Id==id)??throw new InvalidDataException(&quot;Unknown procedure: &quot;+id);
            using var wait=new CancellationTokenSource();
            if(procedure.WaitTimeMs&gt;0)wait.CancelAfter(procedure.WaitTimeMs);
            using var linked=CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(token,wait.Token);
            var runToken=linked.Token;
            async Task&lt;ExecutionSessionResult&gt; Call(NodeDocument node,IReadOnlyDictionary&lt;string,PortValue&gt; values,CancellationToken childToken){
                var body=document.Procedures.Single(p=&gt;p.Id==node.Call!.ProcedureId);
                // Legacy cross-flow calls retain one-shot behavior. Native Group loops own their body.
                if(body.GroupOwner is null)return await Execute(body.Id,values,childToken,depth+1);
                return await Task.Run(async()=&gt;{
                var loop=GroupLoop.Read(node);ExecutionSessionResult? last=null;int completed=0;bool broke=false;int? breakIndex=null;var diagnostics=new List&lt;string&gt;();var accumulator=new GroupOutputAccumulator(body);
                for(long index=loop.Start;index&lt;loop.End;index+=loop.Increment){
                    childToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                    if(index&lt;int.MinValue||index&gt;int.MaxValue)throw new InvalidOperationException(&quot;Independent Group index overflow guard.&quot;);
                    // Independent resource guard; this is not a recovered VM iteration limit.
                    if(completed&gt;=10000)throw new InvalidOperationException(&quot;Independent Group iteration limit is 10000.&quot;);
                    if(GroupBreak.Evaluate(node,body,last,(int)index,diagnostics,values,(name,element)=&gt;accumulator.ReadSelfElement(name,element,last))){broke=true;breakIndex=(int)index;break;}
                    last=await Execute(body.Id,values,childToken,depth+1,(int)index);completed++;
                    if(last.Status==SessionStatus.Success)accumulator.Add(last);
                    if(last.Status!=SessionStatus.Success)break;
                    if(loop.IntervalMs&gt;0)await Task.Delay(loop.IntervalMs,childToken);
                }
                return (last??new ExecutionSessionResult(SessionStatus.Success,[])) with{CompletedGroupIterations=completed,GroupBroke=broke,GroupBreakIndex=breakIndex,GroupDiagnostics=diagnostics.AsReadOnly(),WorkflowOutputs=accumulator.Finish(last),WorkflowDisplays=accumulator.FinishDisplays()};
                },childToken);
            }

	{
		if (sender == null || !(e is ValueEventArgs e2))
		{
			return;
		}
		if ((NeedFixed &amp;&amp; e2.ExecuteCount != FixedExcuteCount) || e2.ExecuteCount == SpareExecuteCount)
		{
			if (e2.ExecuteCount != SpareExecuteCount)
			{
				SpareExecuteCount = e2.ExecuteCount;
				spareImageStore.Clear();
				spareShapeStore.Clear();
			}
			SetRenderDataToStore(sender, e2, spareImageStore, spareShapeStore);
			return;

		{
			BufferExecuteCount = e2.ExecuteCount;
			bufferImageStore.Clear();
			bufferShapeStore.Clear();

		{
			LogHelper.Error(&quot;错误，同一批数据进了两个不同的缓存.BufferExecuteCount = &quot; + BufferExecuteCount);
		}

	{
		if (isSpare)
		{
			Dictionary&lt;string, ImageData&gt; dictionary = displayImageStore;
			Dictionary&lt;object, object&gt; dictionary2 = displayShapeStore;
			displayImageStore = spareImageStore;
			displayShapeStore = spareShapeStore;
			spareImageStore = dictionary;
			spareShapeStore = dictionary2;
		}
		else
		{
			Dictionary&lt;string, ImageData&gt; dictionary3 = displayImageStore;
			Dictionary&lt;object, object&gt; dictionary4 = displayShapeStore;
			displayImageStore = bufferImageStore;
			displayShapeStore = bufferShapeStore;
			bufferImageStore = dictionary3;
			bufferShapeStore = dictionary4;
		}
	}

	{
		int result = 0;
		Math.DivRem((int)executeCount, maxStoreLen, out result);
		uint num = executeCount;
		procedureSendExecuteCount.TryGetValue(proID, out num);
		bool flag = numStoreForSent[result] == numStoreForReceived[result] || executeCount &lt; num;
		if (flag)
		{
			numStoreForReceived[result] = 0;
			numStoreForSent[result] = 0;
		}

	{
		if (!(e is ValueEventArgs e2))
		{
			return;
		}
		try
		{
			if (!(e2.Value is ImvsSdkDefine.IMVS_MODULE_WORK_STAUS iMVS_MODULE_WORK_STAUS))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (iMVS_MODULE_WORK_STAUS.nWorkStatus == 0)
			{
				if ((m_CurrentInfo.RenderDataCenter.IsBusy || isRendering) &amp;&amp; e2.ExecuteCount != m_CurrentInfo.RenderDataCenter.FixedExcuteCount)
				{
					needRenderSignal = e2;
					return;
				}
				if (e2.ExecuteCount != ExecuteCount)
				{
					LogHelper.Error($&quot;不应该进这个分支.args.ExecuteCount = {e2.ExecuteCount}, ExecuteCount = {ExecuteCount}&quot;);
					ExecuteCount = e2.ExecuteCount;
				}
				UpdateRender(e2.ExecuteCount);
			}
			else if (iMVS_MODULE_WORK_STAUS.nWorkStatus == 1)
			{
				ExecuteCount = iMVS_MODULE_WORK_STAUS.nExecuteCount;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			LogHelper.Debug(&quot;Set isReadyToRender True in Exception.&quot;);
		}
	}</pre></details></details><details class="fact"><summary>UpdateRender / RenderThreadPool.AsyncRun闭包 → 流程分支的装饰器身份门与各UI闭包差异</summary><p><a class="badge source-confirmed" href="results-roi.html#edge-V24">results-roi:V24 · source-confirmed</a></p><p>入口图像源null则清isRendering，先BeforeExchange，再把当前m_CurrentInfo作为AsyncRun状态参数。worker先比较moduleDecorator==当前装饰器才Exchange；等待结束后未立即重查身份，随后核!isclose且当前装饰器非null。主图仓须TryGetImage成功且图不同，不核Tag；两个主图Invoke都再比较装饰器身份，不同时清isRendering，相同时赋图/扩展回调并置hasRendered=true，均无关闭复检。回退门是!hasRendered且SelectedImage.DisplayObject为Object，并非image==null；GetValue(uint.MaxValue)所得图变化才Invoke，第二主图Invoke另置needActiveUpdateShapes=true。输入图须!hasRendered、双图、取图成功且图变化，其Invoke只赋当前输入图，不核身份/关闭，也不置hasRendered，因此仍可随后清DisplayStore。列表/树BeginInvoke只核!isclose。worker catch仅日志，无finally清状态；不能把主图身份门推广到整链。</p><p class="gap">待追：线程池/完成回调已有V25–V32有限恢复；完整取消、Worker等待期间切源以及最终呈现仍未知；当前实现generation门不等同原版此分支。</p><details><summary>源码证据 7</summary><p>docs/architecture-recovery/render-store-cil-evidence.json · 8044–8063</p><pre>          &quot;token&quot;: &quot;0x060001b6&quot;,

	{
		if (m_CurrentInfo.ImageDataSource == null)
		{
			isRendering = false;
		}
		m_CurrentInfo.RenderDataCenter.BeforeExchange(executeCount);
		RenderThreadPool.AsyncRun((object moduleDecorator) =&gt;
		{
			try
			{
				if (moduleDecorator == m_CurrentInfo)
				{
					m_CurrentInfo.RenderDataCenter.Exchange(OwnerProcessID, executeCount);
					if (!isclose &amp;&amp; m_CurrentInfo != null)
					{
						bool hasRendered = false;
						if (m_CurrentInfo.RenderDataCenter.TryGetImage(&quot;SelectedImage&quot;, out var image) &amp;&amp; image != m_CurrentInfo.ImageDataSource)
						{
							isRendering = true;
							Dispatcher.Invoke(() =&gt;
							{
								if (moduleDecorator != m_CurrentInfo)
								{
									isRendering = false;
								}
								else
								{
									if (ResultViewAddition == null)
									{
										m_CurrentInfo.ImageDataSource = image;
									}
									else
									{
										ResultViewAddition.OnModuleCallBackArrived(0, image);
									}
									hasRendered = true;
								}
							}, DispatcherPriority.Background);
						}
						if (!hasRendered &amp;&amp; m_CurrentInfo.SelectedImage?.DisplayObject is Apps.XmlParser.Object obj)
						{
							image = obj.GetValue(uint.MaxValue) as ImageData;
							if (image != m_CurrentInfo.ImageDataSource)
							{
								isRendering = true;
								Dispatcher.Invoke(() =&gt;
								{
									if (moduleDecorator != m_CurrentInfo)
									{
										isRendering = false;
									}
									else
									{
										needActiveUpdateShapes = true;
										if (ResultViewAddition == null)
										{
											m_CurrentInfo.ImageDataSource = image;
										}
										else
										{
											ResultViewAddition.OnModuleCallBackArrived(0, image);
										}
										hasRendered = true;
									}
								}, DispatcherPriority.Background);
								LogHelper.Info(&quot;Current image is null.ExecuteCount = &quot; + executeCount);
							}
						}
						if (!hasRendered &amp;&amp; m_CurrentInfo.IsDoubleImageMode &amp;&amp; m_CurrentInfo.RenderDataCenter.TryGetImage(&quot;SelectedInputImage&quot;, out var imageIn) &amp;&amp; imageIn != m_CurrentInfo.ImageDataSourceIn)
						{
							Dispatcher.Invoke(() =&gt;
							{
								m_CurrentInfo.ImageDataSourceIn = imageIn;
							}, DispatcherPriority.Background);
						}
						if (!hasRendered)
						{
							m_CurrentInfo.RenderDataCenter.ClearDisplayStore();
						}
						Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((BeginInvokeDelegate)(() =&gt;
						{
							if (!isclose)
							{
								RefreshDataListResult(datalist);
								RefreshResultTree();
							}
						}));
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				LogHelper.Error(&quot;渲染异常：&quot; + ex.Message + ex.StackTrace);
			}

 {
  while(true){
   T?[]? batch=null;int count=0;

    if(!alive)return;

	{
		try
		{
			Interlocked.Increment(ref _unRedoing);
			if (_undoStack.Count != 0)
			{
				UpdateActionData();
				IUnRedoOperation unRedoOperation = _undoStack.Pop();
				_redoStack.Push(unRedoOperation);
				(unRedoOperation as IBuilder).BuildParams(dispatcher, () =&gt; RecoverStack(_redoStack, _undoStack));
				(unRedoOperation as IBuilder).BuildInStack(isUndoStack: false);
				unRedoOperation.Undo();
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
		finally
		{
			Interlocked.Decrement(ref _unRedoing);
			NotifyProperty();
		}
	}

    {
        LastError = null;
        replayDepth++;
        try
        {
            var source = isUndo ? undo : redo;
            var destination = isUndo ? redo : undo;
            if (source.Count == 0) return false;
            var root = source[^1];
            if (isUndo)
                foreach (var part in root.Parts.Where(part =&gt; !part.IsDataComplete).ToArray()) part.CompleteNewValue();
            source.RemoveAt(source.Count - 1);
            destination.Add(root);
            Trim(destination);
            foreach (var part in Ordered(root, isUndo))
                if (isUndo) part.Undo(); else part.Redo();
            return true;
        }
        catch (Exception error)
        {
            // Original service catches failures: no implicit rollback or safe retry is invented.
            LastError = error;
            return false;
        }
        finally { replayDepth--; }
    }

    static IEnumerable&lt;DocumentHistoryPart&lt;T&gt;&gt; Ordered(DocumentHistoryRoot&lt;T&gt; root, bool undo)</pre></details></details><details class="fact"><summary>ReleaseRoot / ReleaseRetainedPayloads → 清理失败保留所有权与错误</summary><p><a class="badge independent-implementation" href="history-replay.html#edge-C07">history-replay:C07 · independent-implementation</a></p><p>根释放失败记录error并保留root；显式ReleaseRetainedPayloads重试，Clear/Dispose本身不声称所有文件已删除。不是静默循环重试。</p><details><summary>源码证据 1</summary><p>src/VisionEditor.Core/DocumentHistoryEngine.cs · 132–144</p><pre>    void ReleaseRoot(DocumentHistoryRoot&lt;T&gt; root)

	{
		uint num = 0u;
		reMsg = &quot;&quot;;
		string text = &quot;&quot;;
		CMDStatusWithServer cMDStatusWithServer = (CMDStatusWithServer)Convert.ToInt32(cmd);
		if (cMDStatusWithServer == CMDStatusWithServer.SetCommunicateData)
		{
			if (pInMsg == IntPtr.Zero || nMsgLen &lt;= 0)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			objScriptManager.SetCommunicateData(pInMsg);
		}
		else if (pInMsg != IntPtr.Zero &amp;&amp; nMsgLen &gt;= 0)
		{
			byte[] array = new byte[nMsgLen];
			Marshal.Copy(pInMsg, array, 0, nMsgLen);
			text = Encoding.UTF8.GetString(array);
		}
		LogHelper.Info(&quot;Receive:&quot; + cMDStatusWithServer);
		switch (cMDStatusWithServer)
		{
		case CMDStatusWithServer.LoadSolution:
			bNeedLoadDefaultScript = false;
			num = objScriptManager.LoadSolution(text, objServerInfo.IsCrash);
			break;
		case CMDStatusWithServer.SaveSolution:
			num = objScriptManager.SaveSolutionByMap(out reMsg);
			break;
		case CMDStatusWithServer.ExcuteOnce:
			num = objScriptManager.StartOnce();
			break;
		case CMDStatusWithServer.ExcuteContinue:
			num = objScriptManager.StartContinueRun();
			break;
		case CMDStatusWithServer.StopExcute:
			num = objScriptManager.StopExcute();
			break;
		case CMDStatusWithServer.ShowWindow:
			num = 0u;
			break;
		case CMDStatusWithServer.CloseScript:
			objServerInfo.IsCrash = false;
			num = objScriptManager.CloseScript();
			break;
		case CMDStatusWithServer.GetMsgToUI:
			num = objScriptManager.GetMsgToUI(text, ref reMsg);
			break;
		case CMDStatusWithServer.SetMsgFromUI:
			num = objScriptManager.SetMsgFromUI(text);
			break;
		case CMDStatusWithServer.SetVMZmqPair:
			num = objScriptManager.SetVMZmqPair(text);
			break;
		case CMDStatusWithServer.ReleaseSharedMemory:
			num = objScriptManager.ReleaseShaleMap(text);
			break;
		case CMDStatusWithServer.SilentlyExecuteOnce:
			num = objScriptManager.SilentlyExecuteOnce();
			break;
		case CMDStatusWithServer.LoadSolutionEnd:
			num = objScriptManager.ExecuteLoadInit();
			break;
		}
		LogHelper.Info(&quot;Receive end:&quot; + cMDStatusWithServer);
		return 0;
	}
